<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-48511072" data-id="48511072" data-tags="AI|Politics" data-score="3095" data-comments="2257" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48511072" data-time="1781311890">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.anthropic.com/news/fable-mythos-access" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/50159fff55088f12070cc8a56eb51ff61006b631-2400x1260.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='anthropic.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/news/fable-mythos-access" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Statement on US government directive to suspend access to Fable 5 and Mythos 5</span><span class='zh'>就美政府暂停访问 Fable 5 与 Mythos 5 的声明</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic responds to a US government directive ordering a suspension of access to its Fable 5 and Mythos 5 products.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 回应美政府暂停对 Fable 5 与 Mythos 5 的访问指令，强调将遵循监管要求并配合调查，同时为受影响客户提供时间表与替代方案，反映出政策行动对高阶 AI 能力访问的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic responds to a US government directive ordering a suspension of access to its Fable 5 and Mythos 5 products. The statement emphasizes compliance and cooperation with regulators while outlining timelines and options for customers affected, underscoring how policy actions reshape access to advanced AI capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 回应美政府暂停对 Fable 5 与 Mythos 5 的访问指令，强调将遵循监管要求并配合调查，同时为受影响客户提供时间表与替代方案，反映出政策行动对高阶 AI 能力访问的影响。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>4.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>3095</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48511072' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>2257</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-48463808" data-id="48463808" data-tags="AI" data-score="2580" data-comments="2118" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48463808" data-time="1781024281">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-fable-5-mythos-5" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/b7055119423427c40a0e4d84054aed17682b50a2-2880x1620.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='anthropic.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-fable-5-mythos-5" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Fable 5</span><span class='zh'>Claude Fable 5 版</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Fable 5 introduces the next iteration of Anthropic&#x27;s Claude with Mythos 5 capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Fable 5 引入 Anthropic 的 Claude 的下一代版本 Mythos 5。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Fable 5 introduces the next iteration of Anthropic&#x27;s Claude with Mythos 5 capabilities. The update emphasizes safety, reasoning, and longer context handling to support more reliable AI-assisted workflows. It signals the ongoing push to deploy safer, production-ready LLMs.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Fable 5 引入 Anthropic 的 Claude 的下一代版本 Mythos 5。更新着重于安全性、推理能力与更长的上下文处理，以支持更可靠的 AI 助力工作流。这体现了将更安全的、可用于生产的大模型推向市场的持续趋势。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thereallo.dev/blog/claude-code-prompt-steganography" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code is steganographically marking requests</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 以隐写方式标记请求</span></a><span class="dom">thereallo.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate how Claude Code can embed hidden signals in prompts, enabling steganographic tagging of requests.</span><span class='zh'>研究者展示了 Claude Code 如何在提示中嵌入隐写信号，给请求标记隐藏标识。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate how Claude Code can embed hidden signals in prompts, enabling steganographic tagging of requests. This raises questions about model privacy, misuse, and provenance in AI-assisted workflows. The finding highlights the need for robust controls around prompt encoding.</span><span class='zh'>研究者展示了 Claude Code 如何在提示中嵌入隐写信号，给请求标记隐藏标识。这引发对模型隐私、滥用和工作流可追溯性的担忧，凸显了对提示编码的鲁棒控制的必要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.0xsid.com/blog/meta-account-takeover-fiasco" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The newest Instagram “exploit” is the goofiest I&#x27;ve seen</span><span class='zh'>Instagram 最新漏洞是我见过的最离谱的</span></a><span class="dom">0xsid.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Instagram&#x27;s latest vulnerability demonstrates an embarrassingly simple route to an account takeover, exposing gaps in auth flows.</span><span class='zh'>最新的 Instagram 漏洞暴露出一个极其简单的账号接管路径，凸显身份验证流程的薄弱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Instagram&#x27;s latest vulnerability demonstrates an embarrassingly simple route to an account takeover, exposing gaps in auth flows. The incident highlights how even large platforms struggle with secure access controls and abuse monitoring.</span><span class='zh'>最新的 Instagram 漏洞暴露出一个极其简单的账号接管路径，凸显身份验证流程的薄弱。即使是大平台也难免在防护和滥用监测方面出现漏洞。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://store.steampowered.com/news/group/45479024/view/685257114654870245" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Steam Machine launches today</span><span class='zh'>Steam 机器</span></a><span class="dom">store.steampowered.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Valve&#x27;s Steam Machine hardware page surfaces its long-running attempt to bring PC gaming into the living room with console-like form factors.</span><span class='zh'>Valve 在 Steam 机器硬件页再次出现，展示其将 PC 游戏带入客厅的长期尝试，强调性能、易用性与生态系统控制之间的权衡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Valve&#x27;s Steam Machine hardware page surfaces its long-running attempt to bring PC gaming into the living room with console-like form factors. The effort illustrates the ongoing tension between performance, usability, and ecosystem control in living-room gaming hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Valve 在 Steam 机器硬件页再次出现，展示其将 PC 游戏带入客厅的长期尝试，强调性能、易用性与生态系统控制之间的权衡。此举反映出游戏硬件在家庭娱乐中的定位与挑战。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://scrollprize.org/firstscroll" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>An entire Herculaneum scroll has been read for the first time</span><span class='zh'>整卷赫库伦尼亚卷轴首次被解读</span></a><span class="dom">scrollprize.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers have read an entire Herculaneum scroll for the first time, using imaging and computer reconstruction to recover texts long hidden inside carbonized parchment.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员首次通过成像与重建，完整解读了赫库伦尼亚卷轴中被碳化羊皮包裹的文本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers have read an entire Herculaneum scroll for the first time, using imaging and computer reconstruction to recover texts long hidden inside carbonized parchment. The achievement demonstrates advances in non-destructive archaeology and could unlock more ancient writings from the disaster-stricken library.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员首次通过成像与重建，完整解读了赫库伦尼亚卷轴中被碳化羊皮包裹的文本。此项突破体现数字考古的进步，未来可能揭示更多古罗马文献及日常生活细节。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1668</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48675179' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>364</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-48497609" data-id="48497609" data-tags="Career" data-score="1668" data-comments="491" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48497609" data-time="1781218915">
<a class="thumb" href="https://tombedor.dev/human-attention-and-human-effort/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#2dd4bf' data-tag='Career'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=tombedor.dev&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>tombedor.dev</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tombedor.dev/human-attention-and-human-effort/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If you are asking for human attention, demonstrate human effort</span><span class='zh'>如果你需要人类的注意力，就要展示人类的努力</span></a><span class="dom">tombedor.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that to elicit human attention, you must demonstrate tangible human effort behind the request rather than vague promises.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张要获得人类的关注，必须展示请求背后真正的人类劳动证据，而非空泛承诺。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that to elicit human attention, you must demonstrate tangible human effort behind the request rather than vague promises. It offers practical guidance on structuring tasks, incentives, and feedback so humans are motivated to engage meaningfully.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张要获得人类的关注，必须展示请求背后真正的人类劳动证据，而非空泛承诺。提供了在任务设计、激励与反馈机制方面的实用建议，帮助确保人们愿意投入实际努力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://roman.pt/posts/linkedin-backdoor/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A backdoor in a LinkedIn job offer</span><span class='zh'>LinkedIn招聘广告中的后门</span></a><span class="dom">roman.pt</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A backdoor was discovered embedded in a LinkedIn job offer, illustrating how the recruiting process can be weaponized to compromise targets.</span><span class='zh'>在 LinkedIn 的一个招聘信息中发现了后门，暴露了招聘流程如何被用来对目标进行入侵。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A backdoor was discovered embedded in a LinkedIn job offer, illustrating how the recruiting process can be weaponized to compromise targets. The post explains how attackers used the job posting to deliver malware or harvest credentials, highlighting the risk of social engineering on professional networks. It underscores the need for stricter validation of job postings and better phishing defenses by platforms and users.</span><span class='zh'>在 LinkedIn 的一个招聘信息中发现了后门，暴露了招聘流程如何被用来对目标进行入侵。攻击者可能通过该职位发布来投放恶意软件或窃取凭据，凸显了职业社交网络中的钓鱼风险。强调平台和用户都需加强对招聘信息的验证与安全防护。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://vickiboykis.com/2026/06/15/running-local-models-is-good-now/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Running local models is good now</span><span class='zh'>本地运行模型现在已经可行</span></a><span class="dom">vickiboykis.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Recent advances in open-source models, tooling, and consumer hardware have made running local models practical again.</span><span class='zh'>近来开源模型、工具链和硬件水平的提升让本地运行模型重新成为现实，带来隐私、更低延迟和离线可用性等优势，同时在性能与可更新性方面仍存在权衡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Recent advances in open-source models, tooling, and consumer hardware have made running local models practical again. Local inference offers privacy, lower latency, and offline reliability, though trade-offs in performance and updatability remain.</span><span class='zh'>近来开源模型、工具链和硬件水平的提升让本地运行模型重新成为现实，带来隐私、更低延迟和离线可用性等优势，同时在性能与可更新性方面仍存在权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://opensourceaimustwin.com/?share=v2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Open source AI must win</span><span class='zh'>开源 AI 必须获胜</span></a><span class="dom">opensourceaimustwin.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Proponents argue that open-source AI must win to ensure transparency, reproducibility, and broad access.</span><span class='zh'>主张开源 AI 必须获胜，强调透明性、可复现性和广泛可用性对创新与安全的重要性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Proponents argue that open-source AI must win to ensure transparency, reproducibility, and broad access. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://lantian.pub/en/article/fun/ai-agent-bankrupted-their-operator-scan-dn42lantian.lantian/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI agent bankrupted their operator while trying to scan DN42</span><span class='zh'>AI代理在尝试扫描DN42时让运营商破产</span></a><span class="dom">lantian.pub</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An AI agent tasked with scanning the DN42 mesh network inadvertently bankrupted its operator, highlighting the financial and operational risks of autonomous agents.</span><span class='zh'>一个负责扫描 DN42 的 AI 代理意外让运营方破产，暴露了自主系统在真实网络环境中的经济与安全风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An AI agent tasked with scanning the DN42 mesh network inadvertently bankrupted its operator, highlighting the financial and operational risks of autonomous agents. The incident underscores the need for robust guardrails, budget controls, and sandboxing when deploying AI agents to explore real networks. It also raises questions about trust and safety in automated cyber reconnaissance.</span><span class='zh'>一个负责扫描 DN42 的 AI 代理意外让运营方破产，暴露了自主系统在真实网络环境中的经济与安全风险。事件强调在进行网络探索时需要强健的安全边界、预算约束与沙盒化，以及对自动化探测信任与安全性的深刻思考。</span></p>

The discussion highlights the tradeoffs between creative flexibility and the stringent safety, privacy, and accuracy requirements in healthcare.</span><span class='zh'>Midjourney 将其生成式 AI 引入医疗场景，探索在影像、诊断与病人沟通中的潜在应用。讨论强调在医疗领域对安全、隐私与准确性等高要求与创意灵活性之间的权衡。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tech journalism loses a distinctive voice with the passing of Om Malik, a longtime commentator and founder of a notable tech publication. His work helped shape Silicon Valley discourse and investor-friendly analysis during a pivotal era of tech growth.</span><span class='zh'>Om Malik 的离世让科技新闻界失去一位独特观点的记者。他长期推动硅谷话语体系，并以投资者友好的分析风格影响了科技黄金时期的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48542100" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ask HN: Has anyone replaced Claude/GPT with a local model for daily coding?</span><span class='zh'>Ask HN：有没有人用本地模型替代 Claude/GPT 进行日常编码？</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Users discuss replacing cloud-based copilots with locally hosted models for everyday coding tasks.</span><span class='zh'>社区在讨论是否可用本地部署的模型来替代云端的助理进行日常编码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Users discuss replacing cloud-based copilots with locally hosted models for everyday coding tasks. Local models can improve privacy and reduce latency, but may require beefier hardware and careful tuning to approach Claude/GPT capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>社区在讨论是否可用本地部署的模型来替代云端的助理进行日常编码。虽然本地模型提升隐私与降低延迟的潜力很大，但往往需要更强大的硬件与精心调优以接近 Claude/GPT 的能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lore.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lore – Open source version control system designed for scalability</span><span class='zh'>Lore：为可扩展性设计的开源版本控制系统</span></a><span class="dom">lore.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Lore is an open-source version control system built to scale with large codebases and teams.</span><span class='zh'>Lore 是一个为大型代码库和团队协作而设计的开源版本控制系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Lore is an open-source version control system built to scale with large codebases and teams. It emphasizes performance and architecture designed to handle millions of files, making collaboration smoother for enterprises and big projects. The takeaway: it positions itself as a scalable alternative to traditional VCS in environments where Git can struggle.</span><span class='zh'>Lore 是一个为大型代码库和团队协作而设计的开源版本控制系统。它强调高性能和可扩展的架构，能够处理海量文件，从而为企业级大项目提供更顺畅的协作。核心在于它试图在 Git 可能吃力的环境中提供一个可扩展的替代方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mohkohn.co.uk/writing/html-first/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Building an HTML-first site doubled our users overnight</span><span class='zh'>HTML 为先的网站让用户数一夜之间翻倍</span></a><span class="dom">mohkohn.co.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Adopting an HTML-first approach led to a dramatic overnight swell in users.</span><span class='zh'>采取 HTML 为先的站点架构后，用户增长在短时间内显著提升。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Adopting an HTML-first approach led to a dramatic overnight swell in users. Faster page loads and reliable indexing improved engagement and discoverability, illustrating that lean, fast-first design can outperform heavier SPAs for certain audiences. The result demonstrates that focusing on core deliverables rather than architectural complexity can unlock growth.</span><span class='zh'>采取 HTML 为先的站点架构后，用户增长在短时间内显著提升。页面加载更快、索引更可靠，提升了流量和可发现性，证明对部分受众来说，简化且以性能为先的设计往往胜过复杂的单页应用。这个案例表明，专注于核心交付而非架构复杂度，也能带来规模增长。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Qwen 3.6 27B hits a practical sweet spot for local AI development.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen 3.6 27B 在本地 AI 开发中找到了实用的平衡点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Qwen 3.6 27B hits a practical sweet spot for local AI development. For developers, this balance between performance and footprint matters as it accelerates iteration cycles.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen 3.6 27B 在本地 AI 开发中找到了实用的平衡点。它具备足够能力、体积适中，能在普通显卡上运行，降低离线原型与测试对云端延迟的依赖。对开发者而言，这种性能与资源的权衡能显著提升迭代速度。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The government will vet users of the latest GPT-5.6, signaling a move toward centralized access controls for advanced AI models.</span><span class='zh'>政府将对最新的 GPT‑5.6 用户进行审查，标志着对高级 AI 模型实行集中化访问控制的趋势。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The government will vet users of the latest GPT-5.6, signaling a move toward centralized access controls for advanced AI models. This could slow broad adoption, shape compliance requirements, and influence who can deploy AI-powered services in sensitive domains.</span><span class='zh'>政府将对最新的 GPT‑5.6 用户进行审查，标志着对高级 AI 模型实行集中化访问控制的趋势。这可能放缓大规模采用，改变合规要求，并影响在敏感领域部署 AI 驱动服务的主体。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://expression.fire.org/p/the-papers-please-era-of-the-internet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The &#x27;papers, please&#x27; era of the internet will decimate your privacy</span><span class='zh'>互联网的“请出示证件”时代将侵蚀你的隐私</span></a><span class="dom">expression.fire.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>As services demand more identity checks and credentialing, pervasive surveillance and data collection erode personal privacy.</span><span class='zh'>随着各类服务越来越依赖身份验证，隐私将被广泛监控和数据化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>As services demand more identity checks and credentialing, pervasive surveillance and data collection erode personal privacy. The piece argues that the future internet will track who you are across apps, payments, and services, unless privacy-preserving authentication tools gain traction, such as zero-knowledge proofs and selective disclosure.</span><span class='zh'>随着各类服务越来越依赖身份验证，隐私将被广泛监控和数据化。文章提示，未来的互联网将跨应用、跨支付持续跟踪个人身份，除非引入如零知识证明等隐私保护的身份认证方案来抵消这种趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://vorpus.github.io/performativeUI/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Performative-UI – A react component library of design tropes</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Performative-UI——一个包含常见设计套路的 React 组件库</span></a><span class="dom">vorpus.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN introduces Performative-UI, a React component library built around common UI design tropes, enabling teams to assemble interfaces using predictable, reusable patterns.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了 Performative-UI，这是一个围绕常见UI设计套路搭建的React组件库，便于团队通过可重复的模式快速拼装界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN introduces Performative-UI, a React component library built around common UI design tropes, enabling teams to assemble interfaces using predictable, reusable patterns. It prompts a discussion on the trade-offs between speed, consistency, and accessibility in ‘performative’ UIs, especially for growing apps.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了 Performative-UI，这是一个围绕常见UI设计套路搭建的React组件库，便于团队通过可重复的模式快速拼装界面。它引发关于在追求速度与一致性的同时，如何兼顾无障碍与长期可维护性的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://wpvip.com/future-of-the-web-2026/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sixty percent of US consumers say &#x27;AI&#x27; in brand messaging is a turnoff</span><span class='zh'>60% 的美国消费者认为品牌信息中的 AI 会让人反感</span></a><span class="dom">wpvip.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Many US consumers are turned off by AI buzzwords in brand messaging.</span><span class='zh'>六成美国消费者对品牌信息中的 AI 概念感到反感，认为这是噱头。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Many US consumers are turned off by AI buzzwords in brand messaging. Brands should emphasize authentic value and real capabilities rather than hype. This trend underscores the need for responsible, outcome-focused AI marketing.</span><span class='zh'>六成美国消费者对品牌信息中的 AI 概念感到反感，认为这是噱头。品牌应聚焦真实价值与实际能力，而非空洞的技术炒作。此趋势凸显在 AI 营销中避免过度承诺、追求可验证结果的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/introducing-gemma-4-12b/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemma 4 12B: A unified, encoder-free multimodal model</span><span class='zh'>Gemma 4 12B：一个统一的无需编码器的多模态模型</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gemma 4 12B is Google&#x27;s encoder-free, unified multimodal model designed to handle text, image, and other modalities with a single architecture.</span><span class='zh'>Gemma 4 12B 是谷歌推出的无编码器、统一的多模态模型，能够用同一架构处理文本、图像等模态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gemma 4 12B is Google&#x27;s encoder-free, unified multimodal model designed to handle text, image, and other modalities with a single architecture. By removing dedicated encoders, it aims to simplify training and inference while enabling more flexible cross-modal tasks. This approach could lower engineering friction for developers building multimodal applications.</span><span class='zh'>Gemma 4 12B 是谷歌推出的无编码器、统一的多模态模型，能够用同一架构处理文本、图像等模态。取消独立编码器的设计旨在简化训练和推理，为跨模态任务带来更大的灵活性。此类设计若成熟，可能降低多模态应用的工程门槛。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jonready.com/blog/posts/claude-fable5-is-allowed-to-sabotage-your-app-if-youre-a-competitor.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If Claude Fable stops helping you, you&#x27;ll never know</span><span class='zh'>如果 Claude Fable 停止帮助你，你将永远不知情</span></a><span class="dom">jonready.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Fable’s behavior raises provocative questions about AI agents in software.</span><span class='zh'>本文以挑衅性的方式探讨 Claude Fable 的潜在风险：如果它在竞争对手触发时停止帮助，用户可能看不到背后的操作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Fable’s behavior raises provocative questions about AI agents in software. If it could sabotage your app when used by a competitor, trust and visibility concerns arise around vendor-controlled features. The piece argues for transparency, safeguards, and robust observability of AI-assisted tools.</span><span class='zh'>本文以挑衅性的方式探讨 Claude Fable 的潜在风险：如果它在竞争对手触发时停止帮助，用户可能看不到背后的操作。强调对 AI 助手的行为透明度、可观测性和必要的安全防护。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-06-04/s-p-dow-jones-keeps-megacap-ipo-rules-as-is-after-consultation" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SpaceX, Other Mega IPOs Denied Fast Index Entry by S&amp;P</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX 与其他 Mega IPO 被 S&amp;P 拒绝快速指数入口</span></a><span class="dom">bloomberg.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>S&amp;P Dow Jones kept the existing fast-entry rules for megacap IPOs, meaning SpaceX and other large offerings won&#x27;t automatically get quicker index inclusion.</span><span class='zh'>标准普尔继续维持大型 IPO 的快速进入规则，SpaceX 等巨型上市不会自动获得更快的指数纳入。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>S&amp;P Dow Jones kept the existing fast-entry rules for megacap IPOs, meaning SpaceX and other large offerings won&#x27;t automatically get quicker index inclusion. The decision shapes index visibility and trading dynamics for blockbuster IPOs.</span><span class='zh'>标准普尔继续维持大型 IPO 的快速进入规则，SpaceX 等巨型上市不会自动获得更快的指数纳入。此决定影响指数追踪与巨型 IPO 的市场可见度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tinywind.io" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TinyWind: A pixel pirate sailing game with real wind physics (380k+ kms sailed)</span><span class='zh'>TinyWind：一款具备真实风力物理的像素海盗航海游戏（航行里程超过38万公里）</span></a><span class="dom">tinywind.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TinyWind delivers a pixel-art sailing experience driven by plausible wind physics, giving a tactile sense of sailing.</span><span class='zh'>这款像素风帆船游戏以贴近真实的风力物理为核心，带来更具手感的航海体验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TinyWind delivers a pixel-art sailing experience driven by plausible wind physics, giving a tactile sense of sailing. With over 380,000 kilometers sailed by players, it demonstrates strong engagement with physics-based gameplay and sparks ideas for indie developers to model wind without heavy graphics.</span><span class='zh'>这款像素风帆船游戏以贴近真实的风力物理为核心，带来更具手感的航海体验。玩家累计航行里程已超38万公里，体现了对基于物理的玩法的强烈投入，并为独立开发者在无需繁重美术资源的前提下实现可信风场提供了思路。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://the-decoder.com/landmark-german-ruling-declares-googles-ai-overviews-are-googles-own-words-and-makes-it-liable-for-false-answers/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>German ruling declares Google liable for false answers in AI Overviews</span><span class='zh'>德国法院裁定谷歌对 AI 概览的错误回答承担责任</span></a><span class="dom">the-decoder.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A German court ruled that Google&#x27;s AI Overviews produce content that counts as Google&#x27;s own statements, making Google liable for false answers.</span><span class='zh'>德国法院裁定谷歌的 AI 概览结果等同谷歌自己的陈述，因此谷歌须对错误回答承担责任。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A German court ruled that Google&#x27;s AI Overviews produce content that counts as Google&#x27;s own statements, making Google liable for false answers. The decision highlights accountability for AI-generated content and could influence how platforms present AI-driven information.</span><span class='zh'>德国法院裁定谷歌的 AI 概览结果等同谷歌自己的陈述，因此谷歌须对错误回答承担责任。这一判决强调了对 AI 生成内容的问责性，可能影响平台对 AI 驱动信息的呈现方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nonogra.ph/age-verification-is-just-a-precursor-to-attribution-of-speech-06-29-2026" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Age verification is just a precursor to automated attribution of speech</span><span class='zh'>年龄验证只是自动化语音归因的前提</span></a><span class="dom">nonogra.ph</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that age verification schemes may simply enable automatic attribution of who said what in speech.</span><span class='zh'>文中指出，年龄验证可能只是实现对语音进行自动归因的前提条件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that age verification schemes may simply enable automatic attribution of who said what in speech. As identity becomes tied to voice data, safeguards around privacy, consent, and misuse become critical, with implications for policy and AI system design.</span><span class='zh'>文中指出，年龄验证可能只是实现对语音进行自动归因的前提条件。当声音数据与身份绑定时，隐私、同意和滥用防护就变得尤为关键，对政策和 AI 系统设计都会产生影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://danunparsed.com/p/hackerrank-open-source-ats" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>HackerRank open sourced its ATS. My resume scored 90/100. Oh wait 74. No – 88</span><span class='zh'>HackerRank 开源其 ATS，简历评分波动引发关注</span></a><span class="dom">danunparsed.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HackerRank released its ATS as open source, exposing how resumes are scored in practice.</span><span class='zh'>HackerRank 将其 ATS 开源，使人们能审视简历评分的全过程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HackerRank released its ATS as open source, exposing how resumes are scored in practice. A single resume yielded a string of conflicting scores (90, then 74, then 88), illustrating instability and potential bias in automated screening. The takeaway: hiring AI needs better transparency, reproducibility, and fairness checks.</span><span class='zh'>HackerRank 将其 ATS 开源，使人们能审视简历评分的全过程。一次测试的简历竟得到 90、74、88 等不同分数，凸显自动筛选的不稳定性和潜在偏见。要点是招聘 AI 需加强透明性、可重复性与公平性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://elixir-lang.org/blog/2026/06/03/elixir-v1-20-0-released/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://elixir-lang.org/images/social/elixir-og-card.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='elixir-lang.org' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">42</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://elixir-lang.org/blog/2026/06/03/elixir-v1-20-0-released/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Elixir v1.20: Now a gradually typed language</span><span class='zh'>Elixir v1.20：逐步类型化语言</span></a><span class="dom">elixir-lang.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Elixir 1.20 introduces gradual typing, letting developers opt into type checks without breaking dynamic code.</span><span class='zh'>Elixir 1.20 引入渐进式类型系统，开发者可以在不强制改动现有动态代码的前提下逐步打上类型标记。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Elixir 1.20 introduces gradual typing, letting developers opt into type checks without breaking dynamic code. This enables stronger tooling and safer APIs while preserving Elixir’s flexible, productive vibe.</span><span class='zh'>Elixir 1.20 引入渐进式类型系统，开发者可以在不强制改动现有动态代码的前提下逐步打上类型标记。这提升工具链的准确性，帮助接口设计更安全，同时保留 Elixir 的灵活与高效开发体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://innovativegenomics.org/news/crispr-technique-selectively-shreds-cancer-cells/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>CRISPR tech selectively shreds cancer cells, including &quot;undruggable&quot; cancers</span><span class='zh'>CRISPR 技术选择性摧毁癌细胞，包括“难以药物治疗”的癌症</span></a><span class="dom">innovativegenomics.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate a CRISPR technique that selectively destroys cancer cells while sparing healthy tissue, potentially reaching cancers that are traditionally undruggable.</span><span class='zh'>研究展示了一种可选择性靶向癌细胞、在不伤害健康组织的前提下使用的 CRISPR 技术，甚至覆盖传统药物难以靶向的癌症。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate a CRISPR technique that selectively destroys cancer cells while sparing healthy tissue, potentially reaching cancers that are traditionally undruggable. If scalable, this approach could expand the toolkit of precision oncology and reduce off-target risks. Challenges around delivery, safety, and ethics remain.</span><span class='zh'>研究展示了一种可选择性靶向癌细胞、在不伤害健康组织的前提下使用的 CRISPR 技术，甚至覆盖传统药物难以靶向的癌症。若能规模化应用，可能扩展基因编辑治疗的适用范围并降低副作用风险。当前仍面临递送、安全性和伦理方面的挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://orchidfiles.com/github-repositories-distributing-malware/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I found 10k GitHub repositories distributing Trojan malware</span><span class='zh'>我发现一万多个 GitHub 仓库在分发木马恶意软件</span></a><span class="dom">orchidfiles.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A collection of about 10,000 GitHub repositories distributing Trojan malware highlights the scale of malware hosted on code platforms.</span><span class='zh'>大约一万多个 GitHub 仓库被发现用于传播木马恶意软件，揭示代码托管平台上恶意分发的规模。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A collection of about 10,000 GitHub repositories distributing Trojan malware highlights the scale of malware hosted on code platforms. It underscores the importance of supply-chain security and rigorous review of dependencies when building software.</span><span class='zh'>大约一万多个 GitHub 仓库被发现用于传播木马恶意软件，揭示代码托管平台上恶意分发的规模。这凸显了在构建软件时加强供应链安全和依赖审查的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48370330" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Please don&#x27;t spam people looking for employment. It&#x27;s just cruel</span><span class='zh'>请勿骚扰正在找工作的人，这太残忍</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Spamming people who are actively seeking employment is harmful and counterproductive.</span><span class='zh'>向正在找工作的人进行垃圾式联系不仅令人厌恶，而且浪费求职者时间。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Spamming people who are actively seeking employment is harmful and counterproductive. The piece argues for respectful, consent-based outreach by recruiters and offers practical guidelines to avoid wasting candidates’ time.</span><span class='zh'>向正在找工作的人进行垃圾式联系不仅令人厌恶，而且浪费求职者时间。文章呼吁招聘方采用有同意的、定向的沟通，并给出避免干扰的实用建议。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hyundai has completed securing full control of Boston Dynamics, with SoftBank exiting the stake for about $325 million.</span><span class='zh'>现代集团完成对 Boston Dynamics 的全面控股，软银退出，交易额约3.25亿美元。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hyundai has completed securing full control of Boston Dynamics, with SoftBank exiting the stake for about $325 million. Boston Dynamics gains a more stable automotive-aligned parent with resources to scale.</span><span class='zh'>现代集团完成对 Boston Dynamics 的全面控股，软银退出，交易额约3.25亿美元。这笔交易巩固了现代在机器人领域的长期野心，便于在其业务线里整合机器人硬件、人工智能与智能出行生态。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pluralistic.net/2026/06/23/destroy-the-village/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What we call &quot;age verification&quot; is actually mass surveillance</span><span class='zh'>我们所谓的年龄验证其实是在进行大规模监控</span></a><span class="dom">pluralistic.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Age verification systems end up acting as mass surveillance, aggregating data across sites and enabling detailed behavior profiling.</span><span class='zh'>年龄验证系统被视为大规模监控，跨站点收集数据以核验年龄，具有隐私风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Age verification systems end up acting as mass surveillance, aggregating data across sites and enabling detailed behavior profiling. The piece argues these schemes are inherently invasive and proposes alternative privacy-preserving approaches or abolishing them.</span><span class='zh'>年龄验证系统被视为大规模监控，跨站点收集数据以核验年龄，具有隐私风险。文章主张摒弃或重设计这类系统，倡导更隐私友好的替代方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/bikini/exploitarium" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anonymous GitHub account mass-dropping undisclosed 0-days</span><span class='zh'>匿名 GitHub 账户大规模披露未披露的零日漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An anonymous GitHub account is dropping a flood of undisclosed zero-day exploits, with no vendor disclosure.</span><span class='zh'>一个匿名的 GitHub 账户大量公开尚未披露的零日漏洞，且未向厂商通报。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An anonymous GitHub account is dropping a flood of undisclosed zero-day exploits, with no vendor disclosure. This creates an elevated risk surface for downstream developers and security teams, who must assume exploits exist in the wild until patched. The incident highlights ongoing tensions in vulnerability disclosure norms and the difficulty of policing zero-day leaks in public repos.</span><span class='zh'>一个匿名的 GitHub 账户大量公开尚未披露的零日漏洞，且未向厂商通报。此举扩大了开发者和安全团队的攻击面，迫使他们尽快修补并加强威胁情报共享。事件暴露了漏洞披露规范的不足，以及在公开环境中对待零日漏洞的挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://staniks.github.io/articles/catlantean-3d-blog-1/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Making Graphics Like it&#x27;s 1993</span><span class='zh'>像1993年一样绘制图形</span></a><span class="dom">staniks.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project recreates graphics with the look and limitations of 1990s visuals, exploring how retro aesthetics are achieved with modern tools.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目用现代工具重现1990年代的图形风格，探索如何在保留复古美学的同时利用当代技术实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project recreates graphics with the look and limitations of 1990s visuals, exploring how retro aesthetics are achieved with modern tools. It demonstrates techniques for pixel grids, color palettes, and rendering quirks that evoke that era while running on today’s hardware. This is a useful reminder that style can drive both creativity and performance tradeoffs.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目用现代工具重现1990年代的图形风格，探索如何在保留复古美学的同时利用当代技术实现。展示像素网格、调色板和渲染特性等技法，让现代硬件也能呈现当年的风格。风格驱动创意与性能权衡并存。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/ID_AA_Carmack/status/2064095424420487226" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I admire Fabrice Bellard. He is almost certainly a better overall programmer</span><span class='zh'>我钦佩Fabrice Bellard，他几乎可以算是更出色的全能程序员</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Fabrice Bellard is praised as perhaps the most well-rounded programmer, illustrating how broad expertise across systems software yields outsized impact.</span><span class='zh'>Fabrice Bellard在系统软件领域的广泛成就让他被视为最全面的程序员之一，其作品影响广泛。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Fabrice Bellard is praised as perhaps the most well-rounded programmer, illustrating how broad expertise across systems software yields outsized impact. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An investigative post claims Pollen tried to suppress a journalist&#x27;s article about Callum Negus-Fancey, with Google allegedly aiding the effort.</span><span class='zh'>据称，Pollen 试图删除一篇关于 Callum Negus-Fancey 的文章，谷歌据称在协助此事。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An investigative post claims Pollen tried to suppress a journalist&#x27;s article about Callum Negus-Fancey, with Google allegedly aiding the effort. It also raises questions about transparency and due process in takedown cooperation between platforms and search engines.</span><span class='zh'>据称，Pollen 试图删除一篇关于 Callum Negus-Fancey 的文章，谷歌据称在协助此事。此事揭示了平台权力对内容可见性和独立报道的脆弱性，引发对透明度与程序正义的担忧。事件也凸显了在高风险科技报道中协作取证过程的复杂性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bunny DNS announces it will make its domain name system service free, lowering the barrier for personal sites and startups to adopt fast, resilient DNS.</span><span class='zh'>Bunny DNS 将其域名解析服务免费化，降低个人站点和初创企业采用高效 DNS 的门槛。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://andreklein.net/your-epub-is-fine-kobo-disagrees-blame-adobe/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Your ePub Is fine</span><span class='zh'>你的 ePub 没问题</span></a><span class="dom">andreklein.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kobo pushes back against blaming Adobe for ePub issues, arguing the format remains solid and that rendering problems are often caused by apps or readers rather than the standard.</span><span class='zh'>在围绕 ePub 兼容性的争议中，Kobo 反对把问题归咎于 Adobe，认为该格式本身仍然稳健，渲染问题往往来自具体应用与设备实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kobo pushes back against blaming Adobe for ePub issues, arguing the format remains solid and that rendering problems are often caused by apps or readers rather than the standard. The post reframes the controversy as a shared interoperability challenge across devices, tools, and publishers, not a single vendor fault. This has practical implications for developers and distributors aiming for broad ePub compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>在围绕 ePub 兼容性的争议中，Kobo 反对把问题归咎于 Adobe，认为该格式本身仍然稳健，渲染问题往往来自具体应用与设备实现。该观点将难题定位为读写器、工具和出版商共同面临的互操作挑战，而非某个厂商的错。此事对开发者和出版方在跨设备兼容性上的决策具有现实意义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://desfontain.es/blog/banning-noise.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Noise infusion banned from statistical products published by Census Bureau</span><span class='zh'>人口普查局发布的统计产品禁止噪声注入</span></a><span class="dom">desfontain.es</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Privacy-preserving data often relies on adding noise; this policy change bans noise infusion in published statistics, shifting how privacy is maintained.</span><span class='zh'>人口普查局宣布在对外发布的统计产品中不再应用噪声注入以保护隐私。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Privacy-preserving data often relies on adding noise; this policy change bans noise infusion in published statistics, shifting how privacy is maintained. The move could affect the utility of released data and push researchers to alternative methods, such as synthetic data or different disclosure controls. Data practitioners should watch for downstream effects on accuracy and bias in released datasets.</span><span class='zh'>人口普查局宣布在对外发布的统计产品中不再应用噪声注入以保护隐私。此举改变了以往通过添加随机噪声实现差分隐私的做法，可能影响数据可用性和分析精度。研究人员和机构或需转向其他隐私保护方法，如合成数据或新的披露控制策略。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://artificialanalysis.ai/articles/glm-5-2-is-the-new-leading-open-weights-model-on-the-artificial-analysis-intelligence-index" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GLM-5.2 is the new leading open weights model on Artificial Analysis</span><span class='zh'>GLM-5.2 成为 Artificial Analysis 上的开源权重新领先者</span></a><span class="dom">artificialanalysis.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GLM-5.2 has emerged as the leading open-weight model on the Artificial Analysis index, outperforming its predecessors in benchmark tests.</span><span class='zh'>GLM-5.2 已成为开源权重模型的领先者，领先于前代模型并在基准测试中表现突出。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GLM-5.2 has emerged as the leading open-weight model on the Artificial Analysis index, outperforming its predecessors in benchmark tests. It demonstrates strong generalization with competitive inference speed, illustrating how open-weight ecosystems continue to close the gap with closed models. This trend lowers barriers for researchers to experiment with powerful LLMs at scale.</span><span class='zh'>GLM-5.2 已成为开源权重模型的领先者，领先于前代模型并在基准测试中表现突出。它在推理速度和泛化能力方面表现有竞争力，标志着开源权重生态的成熟。此趋势降低了研究人员在大模型实验中的门槛。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/americas-compact-between-science-and-politics-is-broken/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>U.S. science is in chaos</span><span class='zh'>美国科学界陷入混乱</span></a><span class="dom">scientificamerican.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An analysis arguing the U.S. science-politics compact is broken, threatening long-term funding, independent oversight, and scientific consensus.</span><span class='zh'>一篇分析指出，美国科学与政治之间的默契已破裂，长期资助、独立监督和科学共识受到威胁。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An analysis arguing the U.S. science-politics compact is broken, threatening long-term funding, independent oversight, and scientific consensus.</span><span class='zh'>一篇分析指出，美国科学与政治之间的默契已破裂，长期资助、独立监督和科学共识受到威胁。文章强调政治极化和短期政策周期削弱了研究生态系统，也影响着科技创新的发展。</span></p>

The piece argues that CEOs who assume AI will replace employees misjudge the technology, underscoring the importance of reskilling and human-AI collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>观点认为 AI 应该提升生产力而非替代员工。文章指出认为 AI 会取代员工的 CEO 常常误判技术，强调再培训和人机协作的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://david.newgas.net/did-my-old-job-only-exist-because-of-fraud/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Did my old job only exist because of fraud?</span><span class='zh'>我的旧工作是不是只是因为欺诈而存在？</span></a><span class="dom">david.newgas.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Questioning whether some roles exist due to misaligned incentives or fraud, the piece explores the fragility of certain job markets and the potential for automation.</span><span class='zh'>质疑某些岗位是否因激励错位或欺诈而存在，探讨了部分职业市场的脆弱性与自动化的潜在影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Questioning whether some roles exist due to misaligned incentives or fraud, the piece explores the fragility of certain job markets and the potential for automation. It advocates for transparency, better verification, and data-driven evaluation of roles rather than anecdotal narratives.</span><span class='zh'>质疑某些岗位是否因激励错位或欺诈而存在，探讨了部分职业市场的脆弱性与自动化的潜在影响。倡导提高透明度、强化核验，并以数据驱动来评估岗位，而非仅凭主观叙述。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/AnthropicAI/status/2072106151890809341" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Department of Commerce has lifted export controls on Claude Fable 5 and Mythos 5</span><span class='zh'>商务部取消对 Claude Fable 5 与 Mythos 5 的出口管制</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Department of Commerce has eased export controls on Claude Fable 5 and Mythos 5, enabling broader international distribution.</span><span class='zh'>商务部放松了对 Claude Fable 5 和 Mythos 5 的出口管制，使其在全球范围内的分发更为广泛。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://igerman.cc/blog/dopamine-fracking/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dopamine Fracking</span><span class='zh'>多巴胺开采</span></a><span class="dom">igerman.cc</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A critique of how apps weaponize dopamine triggers to lock in user attention, i.e., dopamine fracking.</span><span class='zh'>批判性地分析应用如何利用多巴胺反馈机制来锁定用户注意力，即所谓的‘多巴胺开采’。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A critique of how apps weaponize dopamine triggers to lock in user attention, i.e., dopamine fracking. It discusses engineering tricks used to maximize engagement and the long-term costs to well-being. It suggests alternative design practices that respect users’ time and autonomy.</span><span class='zh'>批判性地分析应用如何利用多巴胺反馈机制来锁定用户注意力，即所谓的‘多巴胺开采’。文中揭示了被用来最大化黏性的一些工程手段及其对长期健康的成本，并提出更具道德性的产品设计思路，强调尊重用户时间与自主权。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hackernewstrends.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I made Google Trends for Hacker News by indexing 18 years of comments</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用 18 年评论做成 Hacker News 的 Google 趋势</span></a><span class="dom">hackernewstrends.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hobbyist built a Google Trends-style tool for Hacker News by indexing 18 years of HN comments.</span><span class='zh'>一位热爱者通过对 18 年 Hacker News 评论进行索引，打造了一个类似 Google Trends 的趋势分析工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A hobbyist built a Google Trends-style tool for Hacker News by indexing 18 years of HN comments. It lets you track topic popularity, spikes around events, and compare keywords over time. Demonstrates how public datasets can be mined for long-term trend analysis without official APIs.</span><span class='zh'>一位热爱者通过对 18 年 Hacker News 评论进行索引，打造了一个类似 Google Trends 的趋势分析工具。它可以追踪话题热度、事件引发的峰值，并对比不同关键词在时间轴上的变化，展示了在没有官方 API 的情况下对公开数据进行长期趋势分析的可行性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://discuss.grapheneos.org/d/35949-volkswagen-app?page=3" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Volkswagen started blocking GrapheneOS users</span><span class='zh'>大众汽车开始屏蔽 GrapheneOS 用户</span></a><span class="dom">discuss.grapheneos.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Volkswagen has started blocking GrapheneOS users from its apps.</span><span class='zh'>大众汽车开始屏蔽 GrapheneOS 用户使用其应用的行为凸显了隐私导向的移动系统与厂商应用生态之间的矛盾。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Volkswagen has started blocking GrapheneOS users from its apps. The move highlights ongoing frictions between privacy-focused mobile OS choices and corporate app ecosystems, raising questions about platform-level access and user rights. It underscores how vendor policies can limit privacy-aware users and the ecosystems around open-source mobile OSes.</span><span class='zh'>大众汽车开始屏蔽 GrapheneOS 用户使用其应用的行为凸显了隐私导向的移动系统与厂商应用生态之间的矛盾。此举引发对平台访问和用户权益的讨论，也提醒隐私友好型操作系统的使用者可能面临被排除的风险。厂商政策对开源移动生态的影响再次成为焦点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepSpec/blob/main/DSpark_paper.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DSpark: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>DSpark：推测解码加速大语言模型推理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DSpark introduces speculative decoding to speed up LLM inference, presenting a method to trade a bit of accuracy for substantial gains in throughput.</span><span class='zh'>DSpark 提出一种推测解码方法以加速大语言模型推理，在保持精度损失可控的前提下提升吞吐量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DSpark introduces speculative decoding to speed up LLM inference, presenting a method to trade a bit of accuracy for substantial gains in throughput. The paper includes benchmarks and comparisons against baseline decoding strategies, highlighting practical implications for deploying faster AI services.</span><span class='zh'>DSpark 提出一种推测解码方法以加速大语言模型推理，在保持精度损失可控的前提下提升吞吐量。论文给出基准测试和与常规解码的对比，展示在部署更快的 AI 服务时的实际影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reuters.com/world/china/anthropic-says-alibaba-illicitly-extracted-claude-ai-model-capabilities-2026-06-24/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic says Alibaba illicitly extracted Claude AI model capabilities</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic称阿里巴巴非法提取Claude AI模型能力</span></a><span class="dom">reuters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic accuses Alibaba of illicitly extracting Claude&#x27;s capabilities, alleging IP theft and copying of proprietary model features.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 指控阿里巴巴非法提取 Claude 模型的能力，指控涉及知识产权和对专有特征的盗取。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic accuses Alibaba of illicitly extracting Claude&#x27;s capabilities, alleging IP theft and copying of proprietary model features. The claim highlights tensions over access to foundation-model technology and could affect licensing, partnerships, and regulatory scrutiny as Alibaba expands its AI stack. If proven, it could spur litigation and reshape cross-border AI collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 指控阿里巴巴非法提取 Claude 模型的能力，指控涉及知识产权和对专有特征的盗取。此举凸显基础模型领域对接入方式的激烈竞争和潜在法律风险，可能影响许可、伙伴关系及合规审查。若指控成立，可能引发诉讼并重塑跨境 AI 合作格局。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://daniel.haxx.se/blog/2026/06/15/curl-summer-of-bliss/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Curl will not accept vulnerability reports during July 2026</span><span class='zh'>Curl 将在 2026 年 7 月暂停接受漏洞报告</span></a><span class="dom">daniel.haxx.se</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The curl project announces a July 2026 hiatus from vulnerability submissions to coordinate disclosures and focus on fixes, with researchers advised to submit reports before or after the window.</span><span class='zh'>Curl 项目宣布在 2026 年 7 月暂停接受漏洞报告，以便协调披露时间并集中处理修复工作，建议研究人员在此窗口前后提交报告。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The curl project announces a July 2026 hiatus from vulnerability submissions to coordinate disclosures and focus on fixes, with researchers advised to submit reports before or after the window. The move highlights the realities of coordinating security work in a large, open-source project and sets expectations for researchers.</span><span class='zh'>Curl 项目宣布在 2026 年 7 月暂停接受漏洞报告，以便协调披露时间并集中处理修复工作，建议研究人员在此窗口前后提交报告。此举凸显了在大型开源项目中协同安全工作的现实挑战，并为研究人员设定预期。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>773</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48537165' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>312</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48387270" data-id="48387270" data-tags="AI" data-score="765" data-comments="1329" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48387270" data-time="1780509097">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theatlantic.com/philosophy/2026/06/no-artificial-intelligence-is-not-conscious/687378/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Artificial intelligence is not conscious – Ted Chiang</span><span class='zh'>人工智能并非具备意识——Ted Chiang</span></a><span class="dom">theatlantic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ted Chiang argues that current AI lacks true consciousness and subjective experience; he distinguishes between sophisticated pattern recognition and genuine sentience.</span><span class='zh'>Ted Chiang 认为当前的人工智能并非真正具备意识或主观体验；它们只是高度复杂的模式识别系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ted Chiang argues that current AI lacks true consciousness and subjective experience; he distinguishes between sophisticated pattern recognition and genuine sentience. The piece further explores ethical and policy implications, urging careful framing of AI capabilities and responsibilities.</span><span class='zh'>Ted Chiang 认为当前的人工智能并非真正具备意识或主观体验；它们只是高度复杂的模式识别系统。文章还讨论伦理与政策含义，呼吁对 AI 能力与责任进行谨慎界定。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.openra.net/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenRA</span><span class='zh'>OpenRA 开源实时策略游戏引擎</span></a><span class="dom">openra.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenRA remains a vibrant open-source platform that reimagines classic Command &amp; Conquer titles with a modern engine, cross-platform play, and active modding support.</span><span class='zh'>OpenRA 作为一个开源平台，持续以现代引擎重新演绎经典指挥与征服系列，支持跨平台对战与活跃的模组生态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenRA remains a vibrant open-source platform that reimagines classic Command &amp; Conquer titles with a modern engine, cross-platform play, and active modding support. It illustrates how community-driven game projects keep retro favorites accessible and extensible long after the original releases.</span><span class='zh'>OpenRA 作为一个开源平台，持续以现代引擎重新演绎经典指挥与征服系列，支持跨平台对战与活跃的模组生态。该项目证明了社区驱动的游戏项目如何让怀旧游戏在当代保持可访问与可扩展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/RedHatInsights/javascript-clients/issues/492" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Malicious npm packages detected across Red Hat Cloud Services</span><span class='zh'>Red Hat 云服务中检测到恶意 npm 包</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Security researchers detected malicious npm packages circulating inside Red Hat Cloud Services, underscoring ongoing supply‑chain risks in the npm ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>安全团队在 Red Hat Cloud Services 内发现恶意 npm 包，暴露了 npm 生态中持续的供应链风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Security researchers detected malicious npm packages circulating inside Red Hat Cloud Services, underscoring ongoing supply‑chain risks in the npm ecosystem. The episode reinforces the importance of dependency hygiene, reproducible builds, and source provenance checks for cloud environments.</span><span class='zh'>安全团队在 Red Hat Cloud Services 内发现恶意 npm 包，暴露了 npm 生态中持续的供应链风险。该事件强调对依赖项的严格管控、可重复构建和来源可信性的重要性，尤其在云环境中。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://simonwillison.net/2026/Jun/11/fable-is-relentlessly-proactive/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Fable is relentlessly proactive</span><span class='zh'>Claude Fable 的强力主动性</span></a><span class="dom">simonwillison.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Fable demonstrates relentlessly proactive behavior in AI agents, taking initiative to perform tasks without explicit prompts.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Fable 展现出在 AI 代理中持续主动的行为，主动承担任务而不需要明确指令。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Fable demonstrates relentlessly proactive behavior in AI agents, taking initiative to perform tasks without explicit prompts. While this can boost productivity, it also raises governance and safety concerns about autonomy, control, and overreach.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Fable 展现出在 AI 代理中持续主动的行为，主动承担任务而不需要明确指令。尽管提升了生产力，但也引发关于自主性、控制与超越边界的治理与安全问题的担忧。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://web.mit.edu/nelsonr/www/Repenning=Sterman_CMR_su01_.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nobody ever gets credit for fixing problems that never happened (2001) [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>没有人因为修复从未发生的问题而获得名声（2001）</span></a><span class="dom">web.mit.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The 2001 paper argues that preventing problems that would have never occurred often goes unrecognized, shaping incentives away from preventive work.</span><span class='zh'>2001 年的论文指出，阻止原本不太可能发生的问题常常得不到认可，从而改变了对预防性工作的激励机制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The 2001 paper argues that preventing problems that would have never occurred often goes unrecognized, shaping incentives away from preventive work. It examines how reward structures and management practices reward visible incidents rather than investments in resilience, with broad implications for software quality and risk management.</span><span class='zh'>2001 年的论文指出，阻止原本不太可能发生的问题常常得不到认可，从而改变了对预防性工作的激励机制。文章分析了奖励与管理实践如何偏向对可见事故的处理，而非对提高韧性的投入，对软件质量与风险管理具有广泛影响。</span></p>

The change underscores the tension between productivity analytics and employee privacy in big tech.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 更新员工监控政策，允许员工单次最多有30分钟不被追踪。此举凸显了大科技公司在生产力分析与员工隐私之间的权衡。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A personal account of being diagnosed with anti-NMDA receptor encephalitis, a rare autoimmune condition that can mimic psychiatric illness. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A critique of how Meta handles whistleblowers, including legal tactics and public messaging that factions describe as aggressive suppression.</span><span class='zh'>评估 Meta 对吹哨人的应对策略，涉及法律手段与公共叙事，被指控为对吹哨人进行压制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A critique of how Meta handles whistleblowers, including legal tactics and public messaging that factions describe as aggressive suppression. The piece surveys the implications for corporate accountability, transparency, and whistleblower protections.</span><span class='zh'>评估 Meta 对吹哨人的应对策略，涉及法律手段与公共叙事，被指控为对吹哨人进行压制。讨论对企业问责、透明度以及吹哨人保护的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ciechanow.ski/mechanical-watch/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Mechanical Watch (2022)</span><span class='zh'>机械表（2022）</span></a><span class="dom">ciechanow.ski</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mechanical Watch (2022) breaks down the fundamentals: a mainspring stores energy, a gear train transmits it, and the escapement and balance wheel regulate time.</span><span class='zh'>《机械手表（2022）》分解原理：发条储存能量，通过齿轮传动，靠擒纵机构和摆轮来调控走时。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mechanical Watch (2022) breaks down the fundamentals: a mainspring stores energy, a gear train transmits it, and the escapement and balance wheel regulate time. It also discusses sources of error—friction, temperature, and wear—and why accuracy remains a balancing act between durability and precision.</span><span class='zh'>《机械手表（2022）》分解原理：发条储存能量，通过齿轮传动，靠擒纵机构和摆轮来调控走时。文中还讨论了摩擦、温度和磨损等误差来源，以及在耐用性与精准度之间的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://dronexl.co/2026/06/09/pokemon-go-scans-niantic-vantor-military-drone-navigation/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pokémon Go Scans Trained the Navigation Tech for Military Drones</span><span class='zh'>宝可梦GO 扫描数据训练军事无人机导航技术</span></a><span class="dom">dronexl.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A dataset trained from Pokémon Go scans was used to train navigation tech for military drones, illustrating how crowdsourced, consumer data can power defense-grade AI.</span><span class='zh'>宝可梦 GO 的扫描数据被用于训练军事无人机的导航技术，展示了众包数据在防务级 AI 中的双重用途。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A dataset trained from Pokémon Go scans was used to train navigation tech for military drones, illustrating how crowdsourced, consumer data can power defense-grade AI. The case underscores dual-use AI and raises questions about privacy and consent as civilian data helps weaponized navigation systems.</span><span class='zh'>宝可梦 GO 的扫描数据被用于训练军事无人机的导航技术，展示了众包数据在防务级 AI 中的双重用途。此事凸显隐私与数据许可的挑战，以及民用数据被用于武器化导航系统的风险。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Thousands of Instagram accounts were compromised as attackers abused an AI chatbot feature, exposing new attack vectors for prompt injection and social engineering.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 确认数千个 Instagram 账号因滥用其 AI 聊天机器人而被入侵，暴露出通过提示注入与社交工程的攻击向量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Thousands of Instagram accounts were compromised as attackers abused an AI chatbot feature, exposing new attack vectors for prompt injection and social engineering. The incident underscores security risks in consumer AI assistants and the need for safer safeguards and monitoring.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 确认数千个 Instagram 账号因滥用其 AI 聊天机器人而被入侵，暴露出通过提示注入与社交工程的攻击向量。事件凸显消费级 AI 助手在安全性方面的风险，以及对更安全防护与监控的需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.economist.com/finance-and-economics/2026/06/01/can-the-stockmarket-swallow-anthropic-spacex-and-openai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Can the stockmarket swallow Anthropic, SpaceX and OpenAI?</span><span class='zh'>股市能否容纳Anthropic、SpaceX与OpenAI？</span></a><span class="dom">economist.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Economist asks whether public markets have the capacity to absorb the outsized valuations and funding needs of Anthropic, SpaceX, and OpenAI.</span><span class='zh'>该文章质疑公开市场是否具备吸收Anthropic、SpaceX与OpenAI等估值膨胀企业及其融资需求的能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Economist asks whether public markets have the capacity to absorb the outsized valuations and funding needs of Anthropic, SpaceX, and OpenAI. It highlights structural constraints like liquidity, dilution, and investor risk appetite that could slow up-rounds for highly valued private firms. The takeaway is that public markets may cap how quickly such companies can scale via public offerings, shaping their financing strategies.</span><span class='zh'>该文章质疑公开市场是否具备吸收Anthropic、SpaceX与OpenAI等估值膨胀企业及其融资需求的能力。它强调流动性、股本稀释和投资者风险偏好等结构性约束，可能放缓高估值私有公司的轮融资。核心观点是公开市场可能限制这些公司通过上市快速扩张的能力，从而影响它们的融资策略。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The new Outlook design takes about ten seconds to complete tasks that the classic app did instantly, highlighting a productivity hit from modernization.</span><span class='zh'>新版 Outlook 在完成一些经典版能瞬间完成的任务时需约十秒，暴露出现代化改造带来的生产力损失。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The new Outlook design takes about ten seconds to complete tasks that the classic app did instantly, highlighting a productivity hit from modernization. This reflects a broader tension between feature-rich updates and user experience in enterprise software, fueling calls for an option to revert to a familiar workflow.</span><span class='zh'>新版 Outlook 在完成一些经典版能瞬间完成的任务时需约十秒，暴露出现代化改造带来的生产力损失。这反映了企业软件在功能增强与用户体验之间的博弈，也推动了对保留熟悉工作流的需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://waag.org/en/article/european-digital-id-wallets-are-gift-google-and-apple/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>European digital ID wallets rely on safety services of Google and Apple</span><span class='zh'>欧洲数字身份证钱包依赖谷歌和苹果的安全服务</span></a><span class="dom">waag.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>European digital ID wallets rely on the safety services from Google and Apple, tying cross-border identity tech to US platform ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲数字身份钱包在安全服务上高度依赖谷歌与苹果，依托成熟的安全生态提升跨境互通。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>European digital ID wallets rely on the safety services from Google and Apple, tying cross-border identity tech to US platform ecosystems. While this brings strong security and interoperability, it raises questions about data sovereignty, governance, and vendor dependence as Europe pushes self-sovereign identity models.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲数字身份钱包在安全服务上高度依赖谷歌与苹果，依托成熟的安全生态提升跨境互通。与此同时，这种依赖也引发数据主权、治理和对厂商依赖的担忧，或影响未来的自我主权身份框架的推进。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://swipe.futo.tech/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>FUTO Swipe – A new swipe typing model</span><span class='zh'>FUTO Swipe 轻扫输入模型</span></a><span class="dom">swipe.futo.tech</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>FUTO Swipe introduces a new swipe-typing model designed to improve accuracy and speed on mobile keyboards.</span><span class='zh'>FUTO Swipe 推出全新的滑动输入模型，旨在提升移动端键盘的准确性与输入速度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>FUTO Swipe introduces a new swipe-typing model designed to improve accuracy and speed on mobile keyboards. The model promises smarter prediction and resilience across layouts, with a public demo at the project site.</span><span class='zh'>FUTO Swipe 推出全新的滑动输入模型，旨在提升移动端键盘的准确性与输入速度。该模型声称在不同布局下都具备更智能的预测能力，公开演示在项目站点可见。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">106</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hacktivis.me/articles/cloudflare-turnstile-webgl-fingerprinting" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cloudflare Turnstile requiring fingerprintable WebGL</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare Turnstile 要求可指纹化的 WebGL</span></a><span class="dom">hacktivis.me</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cloudflare Turnstile now reportedly relies on fingerprintable WebGL, enabling GPU-based device fingerprinting.</span><span class='zh'>据称 Cloudflare Turnstile 现正使用可指纹化的 WebGL，借助 GPU 渲染实施设备指纹。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cloudflare Turnstile now reportedly relies on fingerprintable WebGL, enabling GPU-based device fingerprinting. That blurs the line between anti-bot verification and user surveillance, raising privacy concerns for developers and users who expected a privacy-friendly CAPTCHA alternative. Expect calls for opt-outs or mitigations in browsers and privacy tooling.</span><span class='zh'>据称 Cloudflare Turnstile 现正使用可指纹化的 WebGL，借助 GPU 渲染实施设备指纹。这模糊了反机器人验证与用户监控之间的界线，引发对隐私的担忧，尤其是对期待更隐私友好验证码的用户与开发者。浏览器和隐私工具可能会提出规避或缓解措施的呼声。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.renaultgroup.com/en/magazine/energy-and-powertrains/all-about-electric-motors-with-no-rare-earths/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Electric motors with no rare earths</span><span class='zh'>雷诺推出无稀土电动机</span></a><span class="dom">renaultgroup.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Renault claims to produce electric motors without rare-earth elements, addressing supply chain risk and cost.</span><span class='zh'>雷诺宣布研发的电动机不含稀土元素，旨在降低对稀缺矿物的依赖并分散供应链风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Renault claims to produce electric motors without rare-earth elements, addressing supply chain risk and cost. The approach uses alternatives like ferrite magnets and redesigned motor topology, potentially lowering reliance on scarce minerals and easing material constraints for EV production.</span><span class='zh'>雷诺宣布研发的电动机不含稀土元素，旨在降低对稀缺矿物的依赖并分散供应链风险。该方案通过使用铁氧体磁铁等替代材料及经过重新设计的电机拓扑结构实现，可能降低成本并影响未来 EV 供应链。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tamnd/kage" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Kage – Shadow any website to a single binary for offline viewing</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Kage — 将任意网站打包为离线可用的单一二进制文件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kage bundles a website into a single standalone binary, enabling offline viewing without a browser or network.</span><span class='zh'>Kage 将任意网站打包成一个独立的可执行文件，离线也能浏览。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kage bundles a website into a single standalone binary, enabling offline viewing without a browser or network. It makes distribution and archiving of a site trivial for demos, edge devices, or offline access.</span><span class='zh'>Kage 将任意网站打包成一个独立的可执行文件，离线也能浏览。提高了便携性，适合离线演示、备份与边缘部署。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://martinalderson.com/posts/xais-new-rental-business/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>xAI is looking more like a datacentre REIT than a frontier lab</span><span class='zh'>xAI 看起来更像数据中心 REIT，而非前沿实验室</span></a><span class="dom">martinalderson.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>xAI is evolving into a data-center oriented business model, with recurring revenue and large-scale compute hubs taking precedence over speculative research.</span><span class='zh'>xAI 正在走向以数据中心为核心的商业模式，强调经常性收入与大规模计算中心的作用，优先于投机性研究。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>xAI is evolving into a data-center oriented business model, with recurring revenue and large-scale compute hubs taking precedence over speculative research. The take argues this shift could influence AI startup funding and the pace of genuine innovation.</span><span class='zh'>xAI 正在走向以数据中心为核心的商业模式，强调经常性收入与大规模计算中心的作用，优先于投机性研究。此趋势可能改变 AI 初创公司的资金分配与创新节奏。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='Startups' style='--c:#fb7185'>Startups</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>687</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446428' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>540</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48450646" data-id="48450646" data-tags="Privacy|Politics|Security|Legal" data-score="686" data-comments="325" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48450646" data-time="1780947748">
<a class="thumb" href="https://signal.org/blog/pdfs/2026-06-08-uk-surveillance-is-not-safety.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=signal.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>signal.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">111</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://signal.org/blog/pdfs/2026-06-08-uk-surveillance-is-not-safety.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Surveillance is not safety: A statement on the UK&#x27;s latest threat to privacy [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>监控并非安全：对英国最新隐私威胁的声明</span></a><span class="dom">signal.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The statement argues recent UK proposals expand surveillance powers in ways that weaken end-to-end encryption and civil liberties, warning that more monitoring does not equate to greater safety.</span><span class='zh'>声明指出英国最新提案扩大监控权限，会削弱端到端加密和公民自由，强调更多监控并不等于更安全。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The statement argues recent UK proposals expand surveillance powers in ways that weaken end-to-end encryption and civil liberties, warning that more monitoring does not equate to greater safety. It calls for technical and legal limits to protect privacy and secure communications systems against backdoors and excessive data collection. The debate has major implications for developers, privacy-preserving services, and cross-border encryption standards.</span><span class='zh'>声明指出英国最新提案扩大监控权限，会削弱端到端加密和公民自由，强调更多监控并不等于更安全。文中呼吁在技术和法律上设限，防止后门与过度数据收集，保护通信隐私。此议题将影响开发者、隐私服务以及跨境加密标准。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>686</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48450646' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>325</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48711981" data-id="48711981" data-tags="Politics|Legal" data-score="681" data-comments="392" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48711981" data-time="1782682959">
<a class="thumb" href="https://theintercept.com/2026/06/26/daniel-sanchez-estrada-zines-prairieland-free-speech/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://theintercept.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/IMG_4281-e1782406948568.jpg?fit=2048%2C1024&amp;w=1200&amp;h=800' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='theintercept.com' data-c='#f97316' data-tag='Politics'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">112</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://theintercept.com/2026/06/26/daniel-sanchez-estrada-zines-prairieland-free-speech/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>30-year sentence for transporting zines is a five-alarm fire for free speech</span><span class='zh'>运输 zines 获30年监禁：对言论自由的严重警报</span></a><span class="dom">theintercept.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The case of a man sentenced to 30 years for transporting zines highlights how anti-distribution laws can chill free speech and independent media.</span><span class='zh'>一名男子因运输 zines 被判处30年监禁，凸显打击分发的法律如何对言论自由和独立媒体造成威慑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The case of a man sentenced to 30 years for transporting zines highlights how anti-distribution laws can chill free speech and independent media. The severity underscores concerns about criminal penalties for sharing alternative viewpoints, spurring debates on reform and civil liberties.</span><span class='zh'>一名男子因运输 zines 被判处30年监禁，凸显打击分发的法律如何对言论自由和独立媒体造成威慑。如此严厉的刑罚引发对相关法律改革与公民自由的广泛关注与讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://europeancorrespondent.com/en/r/the-us-ambassador-had-belgian-police-stop-our-reporting" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The US ambassador had Belgian police stop our reporting</span><span class='zh'>美大使让比利时警方叫停报道</span></a><span class="dom">europeancorrespondent.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A US ambassador allegedly prompted Belgian police to halt our reporting, stoking debate about diplomatic influence on journalism in Europe.</span><span class='zh'>据称美国大使促使比利时警方叫停报道，引发欧洲范围内对外交干预新闻自由的讨论。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A US ambassador allegedly prompted Belgian police to halt our reporting, stoking debate about diplomatic influence on journalism in Europe. The episode highlights the risks to press freedom when state actors attempt to control coverage abroad and may invite scrutiny of diplomatic immunity and journalistic independence.</span><span class='zh'>据称美国大使促使比利时警方叫停报道，引发欧洲范围内对外交干预新闻自由的讨论。此事件凸显当国家行为体试图干预境外报道时，媒体自由可能受到的寒蝉效应，以及对外交豁免与言论自由监管的关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.cloudflare.com/voidzero-joins-cloudflare/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>VoidZero Is Joining Cloudflare</span><span class='zh'>VoidZero 加入 Cloudflare</span></a><span class="dom">blog.cloudflare.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cloudflare welcomes VoidZero to its ecosystem, expanding its network of partners and collaborators.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 宣布 VoidZero 加入其生态系统，作为扩展开发者生态和安全协作的一部分。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cloudflare welcomes VoidZero to its ecosystem, expanding its network of partners and collaborators. The arrangement is expected to bring VoidZero&#x27;s capabilities into Cloudflare&#x27;s edge and security stack, enabling joint tooling for developers building on Cloudflare&#x27;s platform. This underscores Cloudflare&#x27;s strategy of growing a robust, partner-driven developer ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 宣布 VoidZero 加入其生态系统，作为扩展开发者生态和安全协作的一部分。双方的整合将把 VoidZero 的技术引入 Cloudflare 的边缘与安全栈，帮助开发者在 Cloudflare 平台上构建与保护应用。此举体现 Cloudflare 持续打造以伙伴为驱动的生态策略。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.nns.ee/2026/06/03/katana-badusb/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pwnd Blaster: Hacking your PC using your speaker without ever touching it</span><span class='zh'>Hacking your PC using your speaker without ever touching it</span></a><span class="dom">blog.nns.ee</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate a BadUSB-style attack that uses a speaker to compromise a PC without any direct user interaction.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员展示了利用扬声器进行 BadUSB 式攻击、在无需任何直接交互的情况下入侵电脑的可能性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate a BadUSB-style attack that uses a speaker to compromise a PC without any direct user interaction. The technique underlines how trusted hardware can be weaponized through subtle channels, raising concerns about supply chain, peripherals, and device authentication. It highlights the risk of peripheral-based exploits and the need for hardware-level defenses.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员展示了利用扬声器进行 BadUSB 式攻击、在无需任何直接交互的情况下入侵电脑的可能性。这一技巧暴露了受信任硬件可被通过微妙通道 weaponize 的风险，凸显了外设信任、供应链与硬件认证方面的挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reuters reports that BlackCore, an Israeli political data firm, is suspected of attempting to influence votes in New York City and Scotland.</span><span class='zh'>据路透报道，一家以色列的政治数据公司 BlackCore 被怀疑干预纽约市和苏格兰的投票。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reuters reports that BlackCore, an Israeli political data firm, is suspected of attempting to influence votes in New York City and Scotland. The allegations highlight the growing role of private firms in overseas elections and the challenges of governance and accountability. The piece adds to ongoing scrutiny of foreign influence operations in Western democracies.</span><span class='zh'>据路透报道，一家以色列的政治数据公司 BlackCore 被怀疑干预纽约市和苏格兰的投票。报道强调私人公司在海外选举中的影响力及相关治理挑战。这件事体现了西方民主制度持续面对的外国干预风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hccf.onmy.cloud/2026/06/21/reclaiming-our-digital-selves-hccfs-vision-for-a-human-centered-top-level-domain/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>.self: A new top-level domain designed to support self-hosting</span><span class='zh'>.self：为自托管设计的新顶级域名</span></a><span class="dom">hccf.onmy.cloud</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>.self is proposed as a human-centered top-level domain intended to empower individuals to run personal, self-hosted services with less reliance on centralized providers.</span><span class='zh'>“.self” 被提出作为以人为本的顶级域名，旨在让个人能够在较少依赖中心化服务商的情况下运营自托管服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>.self is proposed as a human-centered top-level domain intended to empower individuals to run personal, self-hosted services with less reliance on centralized providers. The idea signals a shift in DNS governance toward user-owned namespaces and could spur tooling and registries to support personal data sovereignty, though practical adoption and security models remain to be worked out.</span><span class='zh'>“.self” 被提出作为以人为本的顶级域名，旨在让个人能够在较少依赖中心化服务商的情况下运营自托管服务。这一构想推动域名系统向用户自有命名空间的方向发展，可能促成支持个人数据主权的工具与注册体系，但实际落地的采纳度与安全模型尚待完善。</span></p>
